Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the asymmetric object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 894. The moduli space has dimension at most 782 with 3 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the asymmetric space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 894 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 782. QED.
